7a8b25a)\n- tools: mkimage: propagate error codes from fit_handle_file() (commit 21705d3)\n\nThese commits made mkimage strictly validate that every image name\nreferenced in a configurations node arch/mips/generic/board-serval.its.S — their configurations reference\nramdisk = \"ramdisk\" but no ramdisk image node is ever defined anywhere in the\nFIT source. generic: Remove undefined image 'ramdisk'","message":"MIPS: generic: Remove undefined image 'ramdisk'\n\nAs originally reported by Guenter Roeck [1] and subsequently confirmed by\nme [2], a MIPS allmodconfig build can fail with:\n\nError: configuration 'pcb110' references undefined image 'ramdisk' in property 'ramdisk'\nmake[3]: *** [../arch/mips/boot/Makefile:173: arch/mips/boot/vmlinux.gz.itb] Error 1\nmake[2]: *** [../arch/mips/Makefile:403: vmlinux.gz.itb] Error 2\n\nOr with:\n\nError: configuration 'pcb105' references undefined image 'ramdisk' in property 'ramdisk'\nmake[3]: *** [../arch/mips/boot/Makefile:173: arch/mips/boot/vmlinux.gz.itb] Error 1\nmake[2]: *** [../arch/mips/Makefile:403: vmlinux.gz.itb] Error 2\n\nGuenter's original report made the observation that this was triggered by\nan operating system update since he saw the same errors appear on\npreviously working kernels after the update.\n\nI prompted an LLM with the information available and it provided the\nfollowing additional information:\n\nThis is not a kernel bug — it's a mkimage behavior change in u-boot-tools.\nYour system recently received an upgrade from u-boot-tools 2024.01 =\u003e\n2025.10 (Ubuntu 24.04 noble-security), which included two upstream commits\nfrom June 2025:\n\n- tools: mkimage: validate image references in FIT configurations (commit 7a8b25a)\n- tools: mkimage: propagate error codes from fit_handle_file() (commit 21705d3)\n\nThese commits made mkimage strictly validate that every image name\nreferenced in a configurations node actually exists in the images section.\nPreviously it silently ignored undefined references, now it errors out.\n\nThe offending files in the kernel are arch/mips/generic/board-jaguar2.its.S\nand arch/mips/generic/board-serval.its.S — their configurations reference\nramdisk = \"ramdisk\" but no ramdisk image node is ever defined anywhere in the\nFIT source. This was always technically wrong; the new u-boot-tools 2025.10\njust enforces it.\n\nTo fix the issue, remove the stale ramdisk references from both ITS files.\n\nAssisted-by: LLM\nReported-by: available and it provided the\nfollowing additional information:\n\nThis is not a kernel bug — it's a mkimage behavior change in u-boot-tools.\nYour system recently received an upgrade from u-boot-tools 2024.01 =\u003e\n2025.10 (Ubuntu 24.04 noble-security), which included two upstream commits\nfrom June 2025:\n\n- tools: mkimage: validate image references in FIT configurations (commit 7a8b25a)\n- tools: mkimage: propagate error codes from fit_handle_file() (commit 21705d3)\n\nThese commits made mkimage strictly validate that every image name\nreferenced in a configurations node actually exists in the images section.\nPreviously it silently ignored undefined references, now it errors out.\n\nThe offending files in the kernel are arch/mips/generic/board-jaguar2.its.S\nand arch/mips/generic/board-serval.its.S — their configurations reference\nramdisk = \"ramdisk\" but no ramdisk image node is ever defined anywhere in the\nFIT source. That\nfixes AHB device recovery, but breaks PCI device suspend/resume. During\nsuspend, IRQs are disabled once in ath11k_core_suspend_late(). Then during\nresume, ath11k_core_reconfigure_on_crash() sees ab-\u003eis_reset clear and\ndisables them a second time. Those IRQs are enabled only once during\nthe following reinitialization, leaving the IRQ lines masked. As a result,\nHTC control responses never arrive, and resume fails:\n\n  failed to receive control response completion, polling..\n  failed to connect to HTT: -110\n  failed to reconfigure driver on crash recovery\n  failed to resume core: -110\n\nFix this by disabling the IRQs unconditionally at the top of\nath11k_core_reconfigure_on_crash(), and instead drop the disable\ncalls from the two places that already disable IRQs before this\nfunction can run: ath11k_core_suspend_late() and ath11k_core_reset().\nThis keeps a single disable per recovery/suspend cycle regardless of\nwhich path (recovery or suspend) triggers it, so the refcount on\nPCI parts stays balanced while AHB parts keep getting the disable\nthat the original commit needed.\n\nFixes: f7a74e131d3f (\"wifi: ath11k: disable interrupts during firmware crash recovery\")\nReported-by: Marek Szyprowski \u003cm.szyprowski@samsung.com\u003e\nCloses: https://lore.kernel.org/linux-wireless/a068a857-a549-4cc2-ae0f-3ef119d3a859@samsung.com/\nTested-by: Marek Szyprowski \u003cm.szyprowski@samsung.com\u003e\nTested-by: Julius Bairaktaris generic: Remove undefined image 'ramdisk'","message":"MIPS: generic: Remove undefined image 'ramdisk'\n\nAs originally reported by Guenter Roeck [1] and subsequently confirmed by\nme [2], a MIPS allmodconfig build can fail with:\n\nError: configuration 'pcb110' references undefined image 'ramdisk' in property 'ramdisk'\nmake[3]: *** [../arch/mips/boot/Makefile:173: arch/mips/boot/vmlinux.gz.itb] Error 1\nmake[2]: *** [../arch/mips/Makefile:403: vmlinux.gz.itb] Error 2\n\nOr with:\n\nError: configuration 'pcb105' references undefined image 'ramdisk' in property 'ramdisk'\nmake[3]: *** [../arch/mips/boot/Makefile:173: arch/mips/boot/vmlinux.gz.itb] Error 1\nmake[2]: *** [../arch/mips/Makefile:403: vmlinux.gz.itb] Error 2\n\nGuenter's original report made the observation that this was triggered by\nan operating system update since he saw the same errors appear on\npreviously working kernels after the update.\n\nI prompted an LLM with the information available and it provided the\nfollowing additional information:\n\nThis is not a kernel bug — it's a mkimage behavior change in u-boot-tools.\nYour system recently received an upgrade from u-boot-tools 2024.01 =\u003e\n2025.10 (Ubuntu 24.04 noble-security), which included two upstream commits\nfrom June 2025:\n\n- tools: mkimage: validate image references in FIT configurations (commit 7a8b25a)\n- tools: mkimage: propagate error codes from fit_handle_file() (commit 21705d3)\n\nThese commits made mkimage strictly validate that every image name\nreferenced in a configurations node actually exists in the images section.\nPreviously it silently ignored undefined references, now it errors out.\n\nThe offending files in the kernel are arch/mips/generic/board-jaguar2.its.S\nand arch/mips/generic/board-serval.its.S — their configurations reference\nramdisk = \"ramdisk\" but no ramdisk image node is ever defined anywhere in the\nFIT source. This was always technically wrong; the new u-boot-tools 2025.10\njust enforces it.\n\nTo fix the issue, remove the stale ramdisk references from both ITS files.\n\nAssisted-by: LLM\nReported-by: ath9k_htc: bound TX aggregation to MAX_TX_BUF_SIZE","message":"wifi: ath9k_htc: bound TX aggregation to MAX_TX_BUF_SIZE\n\n__hif_usb_tx() dequeues up to MAX_TX_AGGR_NUM (20) frames into a\nsingle tx_buf of MAX_TX_BUF_SIZE (32768) bytes, limiting the batch\nby record count but never by cumulative byte length.\n\nWith large frames (MTU 2304), 20 aggregated frames of 2292 bytes\neach exceed the allocation (20 * 2296 = 45920 bytes), so the\nmemcpy() in the loop writes up to 13152 bytes past tx_buf-\u003ebuf\nbefore usb_submit_urb().\n\nPeek the queue head and stop before copying any record that would\ncross MAX_TX_BUF_SIZE, then dispatch the current batch. Leftover\nskbs remain queued and are drained on the next URB completion.\n\nThe byte bound changes the loop's exit semantics: it can now exit\nbefore i == tx_skb_cnt - 1. Stock only finalized tx_buf-\u003elen on\nthat last index (len += offset), so an early break would submit a\nURB holding only the last record's length while every dequeued skb\nis freed on completion, silently dropping frames. Make tx_buf-\u003elen\na running total and advance tx_buf-\u003eoffset per record instead; the\nstride round_up(nskb-\u003elen + 4, 4) is identical to the stock stride\nwhen the loop runs to completion.\n\nTested on hardware with an MTU 2304 flood: the loop stops at\nrecord 15 (len = 32144, offset = 32144, within 32768), no\noversized URB is submitted, and MTU 1500 pings pass 20/20.\n\nFixes: fb9987d0f748 (\"ath9k_htc: Support for AR9271 chipset.\")\nSigned-off-by: Georgios Karantzas \u003cgck.kara@gmail.com\u003e\nAcked-by: Toke Høiland-Jørgensen \u003ctoke@toke.dk\u003e\nLink: https://patch.msgid.link/20260911001143.4507-1-gck.kara@gmail.com\nSigned-off-by: Jeff Johnson \u003cjeff.johnson@oss.qualcomm.com\u003e\n","author_name":"Georgios Karantzas","author_email":"gck.kara@gmail.com","authored_date":"2026-09-11T03:11:43.000+03:00","committer_name":"Jeff Johnson","committer_email":"jeff.johnson@oss.qualcomm.com","committed_date":"2026-09-21T10:58:00.000-07:00","trailers":{},"extended_trailers":{},"web_url":"https://git.codelinaro.org/clo/la/kernel/msm-4.9/-/commit/aeaca27c1db3106967ffbb2b517d98c835f6d500"},"release":null,"protected":false,"created_at":"2026-09-22T01:22:00.000Z"},{"name":"ath-202609212052","message":"","target":"7e5290ad5884bcda690bba4e5ba818ac4b7f7026","commit":{"id":"7e5290ad5884bcda690bba4e5ba818ac4b7f7026","short_id":"7e5290ad","created_at":"2026-09-21T13:56:49.000-07:00","parent_ids":["56e1b12e707ca38907a1f1a1fe8811d7e31dd043"],"title":"net: qrtr: resend HELLO on MHI resume","message":"net: qrtr: resend HELLO on MHI resume\n\nSince the MHI HELLO exchange was relocated, it is sent only at device\nregistration. ath12k: add support to load shared firmware on multiPD","message":"wifi: ath12k: add support to load shared firmware on multiPD\n\nIPQ5332 platform uses a MultiPD architecture where multiple firmware\ninstances share common read-only code segments. The driver currently\nloads only the per-UserPD firmware, which leaves the shared read-only\nfirmware unloaded.\n\nAdd support for loading the shared read-only firmware once before the\nUserPDs are powered up. The shared segment contains common code that is\nidentical across all UserPDs and reduces the total firmware memory\nrequirement.\n\nStore the reserved memory mapping in struct ath12k_ahb_rproc_info so it can\nbe shared by all UserPDs and mapped only once. Keep the mapping valid\nacross UserPD power cycles, and unmap it when the last UserPD is removed.\n\nShut down the shared firmware from the rproc deconfiguration path when the\nlast UserPD is removed.\n\nAlso, pass NULL for the reloc_base parameter because the driver uses fixed\nmemory regions configured through device tree and does not need the\nadjusted physical address after relocation.\n\nTested-on: IPQ5332 hw1.0 AHB WLAN.WBE.1.6-01275-QCAHKSWPL_SILICONZ-1\n\nSigned-off-by: Aaradhana Sahu \u003caaradhana.sahu@oss.qualcomm.com\u003e\nReviewed-by: Baochen Qiang
